# [CHAPTER 22](#toc.xhtml#c22)

# [Self-Assessment 2](#toc.xhtml#c22)

# [Objective](#toc.xhtml#s274a)

This chapter is devised for you to understand your preparation level for CEH certification and application security in general. The assessment is based on the actual questions that are asked in the certification exam and can form an excellent source of revision.

# [Instructions](#toc.xhtml#s275a)

*   The assessment comprises of 125 multiple choice questions.
*   The assessment is to be completed in a duration of 4 hours.
*   Each question carries one mark with no negative marking.
*   Passing score is 85% or 106 correct answers.
*   The answer key is provided at the end of the assessment.

# [Assessment](#toc.xhtml#s276a)

1.  **Based on the below log, which of the following sentences are true?**
    
    **Mar 1, 2016, 7:33:28 AM 10.240.250.23 – 54373 10.249.253.15 – 22 tcp\_ip**
    
    1.  SSH communications are encrypted and it is impossible to know who is the client or the server.
    2.  The application is FTP, 10.240.250.23 is the client, and 10.249.253.15 is the server.
    3.  The application is SSH, 10.240.250.23 is the client, and 10.249.253.15 is the server.
    4.  The application is SSH, 10.240.250.23 is the server, and 10.249.253.15 is the server.
2.  **Darius is analyzing IDS logs. During the investigation, he noticed that there was nothing suspicious found and an alert was triggered on the normal web application traffic. He can mark this alert as:** 
    1.  False-Negative
    2.  False-Positive
    3.  True-Positive
    4.  False-Signature
3.  **What is the main security service a cryptographic hash provides?** 
    1.  Integrity and ease of computation
    2.  Message authentication and collision resistance
    3.  Integrity and collision resistance
    4.  Integrity and computational infeasibility
4.  **Insecure direct object reference is a type of vulnerability where the application does not verify if the user is authorized to access the internal object via its name or key.**
    
    **Suppose a malicious user named Rob tries to get access to the account of a benign user named Ned.**
    
    **Which of the following requests best illustrates an attempt to exploit an insecure direct object reference vulnerability?**
    
    1.  “GET/restricted/goldtransfer?to=Rob&from=1 or 1=1’ HTTP/1.1Host: westbank.com”
    2.  “GET/restricted/accounts/?name=Ned HTTP/1.1 Host: westbank.com”
    3.  “GET/restricted/bank.getaccount(‘Ned’) HTTP/1.1 Host: westbank.com”
    4.  “GET/restricted/\\r\\n\\%00account%00Ned%00access HTTP/1.1 Host: westbank.com”
5.  **Identify the web application attack where attackers exploit vulnerabilities in dynamically-generated web pages to inject a client-side script into the web pages viewed by other users.** 
    1.  SQL injection attack
    2.  Cross-Site Scripting (XSS)
    3.  LDAP injection attack
    4.  Cross-Site Request Forgery (CSRF)
6.  **Bob, your senior colleague, has sent you a mail regarding a deal with one of the clients. You are requested to accept the offer and you oblige. After 2 days, Bob denies that he had ever sent a mail. What do you want to "know" to prove yourself that it was Bob who had sent a mail?** 
    1.  Authentication
    2.  Confidentiality
    3.  Integrity
    4.  Non-repudiation
7.  **You need to deploy a new web-based software package for your organization. The package requires three separate servers and needs to be available on the internet. What is the recommended architecture in terms of server placement?** 
    1.  All three servers need to be placed internally
    2.  A web server facing the internet, an application server on the internal network, a database server on the internal network
    3.  A web server and a database server facing the internet, an application server on the internal network
    4.  All three servers need to face the internet so that they can communicate among themselves
8.  **An analyst is investigating proxy logs and found out that one of the internal users visited website storing suspicious Java scripts. After opening one of them, he noticed that it was difficult to understand the code and that all codes differed from the typical Java script. What is the name of this technique to hide the code and extend analysis time?** 
    1.  Encryption
    2.  Code encoding
    3.  Obfuscation
    4.  Steganography
9.  **Which of the following Bluetooth hacking techniques does an attacker use to send messages to users without the recipient’s consent and similar to email spamming?** 
    1.  Bluesmacking
    2.  Bluesniffing
    3.  Bluesnarfing
    4.  Bluejacking
10.  **You are a penetration tester and are assigned to scan a server. You need to use a scanning technique wherein the TCP Header is split into many packets so that it becomes difficult to detect what the packets are meant for.**
     
     **Which of the following scanning techniques will you use?**
     
     1.  ACK flag scanning
     2.  TCP scanning
     3.  IP Fragment scanning
     4.  Inverse TCP flag scanning
11.  **A hacker named Jack is trying to compromise a bank’s computer system. He needs to know the operating system of that computer to launch further attacks. What process would help him?** 
     1.  Banner grabbing
     2.  IDLE/IPID scanning
     3.  SSDP scanning
     4.  UDP scanning
12.  **In which of the following cryptography attack methods does the attacker make a series of interactive queries, thereby choosing subsequent plaintexts based on the information from the previous encryptions?** 
     1.  Chosen-plaintext attack
     2.  Ciphertext-only attack
     3.  Adaptive chosen-plaintext attack
     4.  Known-plaintext attack
13.  **The network administrator for a company is setting up a website with e-commerce capabilities. Packet sniffing is a concern because credit card information will be sent electronically over the internet. Customers visiting the site will need to encrypt the data with HTTPS. Which type of certificate is used to encrypt and decrypt the data?** 
     1.  Asymmetric
     2.  Confidential
     3.  Symmetric
     4.  Non-confidential
14.  **When utilizing technical assessment methods to assess the security posture of a network, which of the following techniques would be most effective in determining whether an end-user security training would be beneficial?** 
     1.  Vulnerability scanning
     2.  Social engineering
     3.  Application security testing
     4.  Network sniffing
15.  **While conducting a penetration test, the tester determines that there is a firewall between the tester's machine and the target machine. The firewall is only monitoring the TCP handshaking of packets at the session layer of the OSI model. Which type of firewall is the tester trying to traverse?** 
     1.  Packet-filtering firewall
     2.  Application-level firewall
     3.  Circuit-level gateway firewall
     4.  Stateful multilayer inspection firewall
16.  **A hacker is attempting to use nslookup to query Domain Name Service (DNS). The hacker uses the nslookup interactive mode for the search. Which command should the hacker type into the command shell to request the appropriate records?** 
     1.  Locate type=ns
     2.  Request type=ns
     3.  Set type=ns
     4.  Transfer type=ns
17.  **If the final set of security controls does not eliminate all risks in a system, what could be done next?** 
     1.  Continue to apply controls until there is zero risk.
     2.  Ignore any remaining risk.
     3.  If the residual risk is low enough, it can be accepted.
     4.  Remove the current controls since they are not completely effective.
18.  **Which of the following types of firewalls inspects only the header information in a network traffic?** 
     1.  Packet filter
     2.  Stateful inspection
     3.  Circuit-level gateway
     4.  Application-level gateway
19.  **Fingerprinting VPN firewalls is possible with which of the following tools?** 
     1.  Angry IP
     2.  Nikto
     3.  Ike-scan
     4.  Arp-scan
20.  **A newly discovered flaw in a software application would be considered which kind of security vulnerability?** 
     1.  Input validation flaw
     2.  HTTP header injection vulnerability
     3.  0-day vulnerability
     4.  Time-to-check to time-to-use flaw
21.  **Which type of scan measures a person's external features through a digital video camera?** 
     1.  Iris scan
     2.  Retinal scan
     3.  Facial recognition scan
     4.  Signature kinetics scan
22.  **Which of the following conditions must be given to allow a tester to exploit a Cross-site Request Forgery (CSRF) vulnerable web application?** 
     1.  The victim user must open the malicious link with an Internet Explorer prior to version 8.
     2.  The session cookies generated by the application do not have the HttpOnly flag set.
     3.  The victim user must open the malicious link with a Firefox prior to version 3.
     4.  The web application should not use random tokens.
23.  **Which technical characteristic do Ethereal/Wireshark, TCPDump, and Snort have in common?** 
     1.  They are written in Java.
     2.  They send alerts to security monitors.
     3.  They use the same packet analysis engine.
     4.  They use the same packet capture utility.
24.  **Which of the following basic configuration of snort as a network intrusion detection system is required?** 
     1.  Limit the packets captured to the snort the configuration file.
     2.  Capture every packet on the network segment.
     3.  Limit the packets captured to a single segment.
     4.  Limit the packets captured to the /var/log/snort directory.
25.  **A network administrator received an administrative alert at 3:00 a.m. from the intrusion detection system. The alert was generated because a large number of packets were coming into the network over ports 20 and 21. During analysis, there were no signs of attack on the FTP servers. How should the administrator classify this situation?** 
     1.  True negatives
     2.  False negatives
     3.  True positives
     4.  False positives
26.  **Which of the following programming languages is most vulnerable to buffer overflow attacks?** 
     1.  Perl
     2.  C++
     3.  Python
     4.  Java
27.  **A hacker searches in Google for filetype:pcf to find Cisco VPN config files. Those files may contain connectivity passwords that can be decoded with which of the following?** 
     1.  Cupp
     2.  Nessus
     3.  Cain and Abel
     4.  John he Ripper Pro
28.  **An engineer is learning to write exploits in C++ and is using the exploit tool Backtrack. The engineer wants to compile the newest C++ exploit and name it calc.exe. Which command would the engineer use to accomplish this?** 
     1.  g++ hackersExploit.cpp -o calc.exe
     2.  g++ hackersExploit.py -o calc.exe
     3.  g++ -i hackersExploit.pl -o calc.exe
     4.  g++ --compile –i hackersExploit.cpp -o calc.exe
29.  **Which of the following scanning tools is specifically designed to find potential exploits in Microsoft Windows products?** 
     1.  Microsoft Security Baseline Analyzer
     2.  Retina
     3.  Core Impact
     4.  Microsoft Baseline Security Analyzer
30.  **During a penetration test, a tester finds that the web application being analyzed is vulnerable to cross-site scripting (XSS). Which of the following conditions must be met to exploit this vulnerability?** 
     1.  The web application does not have the secure flag set.
     2.  The session cookies do not have the HttpOnly flag set.
     3.  The victim user should not have an endpoint security solution.
     4.  The victim's browser must have ActiveX technology enabled.
31.  **What is the correct PCAP filter to capture all TCP traffic going to or from the host 192.168.0.125 on port 25?** 
     1.  tcp.src == 25 and ip.host == 192.168.0.125
     2.  host 192.168.0.125:25
     3.  port 25 and host 192.168.0.125
     4.  tcp.port == 25 and ip.host == 192.168.0.125
32.  **Which statement is TRUE regarding network firewalls preventing web application attacks?** 
     1.  Network firewalls can prevent attacks because they can detect malicious HTTP traffic.
     2.  Network firewalls cannot prevent attacks because ports 80 and 443 must be opened.
     3.  Network firewalls can prevent attacks if they are properly configured.
     4.  Network firewalls cannot prevent attacks because they are too complex to configure.
33.  **What information should an IT system analysis provide to the risk assessor?** 
     1.  Management buy-in
     2.  Threat statement
     3.  Security architecture
     4.  Impact analysis
34.  **When does Payment Card Industry Data Security Standard (PCI-DSS) require organizations to perform external and internal penetration testing?** 
     1.  At least once a year and after any significant upgrade or modification
     2.  At least once every three years or after any significant upgrade or modification
     3.  At least twice a year or after any significant upgrade or modification
     4.  At least once every two years and after any significant upgrade or modification
35.  **Which of the following describes a component of Public Key Infrastructure (PKI) where a copy of a private key is stored to provide third-party access and to facilitate recovery operations?** 
     1.  Key registry
     2.  Recovery agent
     3.  Directory
     4.  Key escrow
36.  **Suppose an e-commerce site was put into a live environment and the programmers failed to remove the secret entry point that was used during the application development. What is this secret entry point known as?** 
     1.  SDLC process
     2.  Honeypot
     3.  SQL injection
     4.  Trapdoor
37.  **Which of the following is a common Service Oriented Architecture (SOA) vulnerability?** 
     1.  Cross-site scripting
     2.  SQL injection
     3.  VPath injection
     4.  XML denial of service issues
38.  **Which initial procedure should an ethical hacker perform after being brought into an organization?** 
     1.  Begin security testing.
     2.  Turn over deliverables.
     3.  Sign a formal contract with non-disclosure.
     4.  Assess what the organization is trying to protect.
39.  **Which United States legislation mandates that the Chief Executive Officer (CEO) and the Chief Financial Officer (CFO) must sign statements verifying the completeness and accuracy of financial reports?** 
     1.  Sarbanes-Oxley Act (SOX)
     2.  Gramm-Leach-Bliley Act (GLBA)
     3.  Fair and Accurate Credit Transactions Act (FACTA)
     4.  Federal Information Security Management Act (FISMA)
40.  **An IT security engineer notices that the company’s web server is currently being hacked. What should the engineer do next?** 
     1.  Unplug the network connection on the company’s web server.
     2.  Determine the origin of the attack and launch a counterattack.
     3.  Record as much information as possible from the attack.
     4.  Perform a system restart on the company’s web server.
41.  **Which cipher encrypts the plain text digit (bit or byte) one by one?** 
     1.  Classical cipher
     2.  Block cipher
     3.  Modern cipher
     4.  Stream cipher
42.  **You just set up a security system in your network. In what kind of system would you find the following string of characters used as a rule within its configuration? alert tcp any -> 192.168.100.0/24 21 (msg: "FTP on the network!";)** 
     1.  An Intrusion Detection System
     2.  A firewall IPTable
     3.  A Router IPTable
     4.  FTP server rule
43.  **Your company was hired by a small healthcare provider to perform a technical assessment on the network. What is the best approach for discovering vulnerabilities on a Windows-based computer?** 
     1.  Use a scan tool like Nessus.
     2.  Use the built-in Windows Update tool.
     3.  Check MITRE.org for the latest list of CVE findings.
     4.  Create a disk image of a clean Windows installation.
44.  **Which of the following is designed to identify malicious attempts to penetrate systems?** 
     1.  Intrusion Detection System
     2.  Firewall
     3.  Proxy
     4.  Router
45.  **Which of the following is a passive wireless packet analyzer that works on Linux-based systems?** 
     1.  Burp Suite
     2.  OpenVAS
     3.  tshark
     4.  Kismet
46.  **\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ is a set of extensions to DNS that provide origin authentication of DNS data to resolvers and in turn reduce the threat of DNS poisoning, spoofing, and similar attack types.** 
     1.  DNSSEC
     2.  Zone transfer
     3.  Resource transfer
     4.  Resource records
47.  **Which of the following is considered the best way to protect Personally Identifiable Information (PII) from web application vulnerabilities?** 
     1.  Use cryptographic storage to store all PII.
     2.  Use encrypted communication protocols to transmit PII.
     3.  Use a full-disk encryption on all hard drives to protect PII.
     4.  Use a security token to log into all web applications that use PII.
48.  **What is not a PCI compliance recommendation?** 
     1.  Limit access to the card holder data to as few individuals as possible.
     2.  Use encryption to protect all transmission of the card holder data over any public network.
     3.  Rotate employees handling credit card transactions on a yearly basis to different departments.
     4.  Use a firewall between the public network and the payment card data.
49.  **Which of the following BEST describes the mechanism of a Boot Sector Virus?** 
     1.  Moves the MBR to another location on the hard disk and copies itself to the original location of the MBR
     2.  Moves the MBR to another location on RAM and copies itself to the original location of the MBR
     3.  Overwrites the original MBR and only executes the new virus code
     4.  Modifies directory table entries so that the directory entries point to the virus code instead of the actual program
50.  **You want to analyze packets on your wireless network. Which program would you use?** 
     1.  Wireshark with Airpcap
     2.  Airsnort with Airpcap
     3.  Wireshark with Winpcap
     4.  Ethereal with Winpcap
51.  **What is the proper response for a NULL scan if the port is closed?** 
     1.  SYN
     2.  FIN
     3.  PSH
     4.  RST
52.  **Elliot is in the process of exploiting a web application that uses SQL as a back-end database. He’s determined that the application is vulnerable to SQL injection, and has introduced conditional timing delays into injected queries to determine whether they are successful. What type of SQL injection is Elliot most likely performing?** 
     1.  Error-based SQL injection
     2.  Blind SQL injection
     3.  Union-based SQL injection
     4.  NoSQL injection
53.  **Which of the following algorithms can be used to guarantee the integrity of messages being sent, in transit, or stored?** 
     1.  Symmetric algorithms
     2.  Asymmetric algorithms
     3.  Hashing algorithms
     4.  Integrity algorithms
54.  **You are performing a penetration test for a client and have gained shell access to a Windows machine on the internal network. You intend to retrieve all the DNS records for the internal domain. If the DNS server is at 192.168.10.2 and the domain name is abccorp.local, what command would you type at the nslookup prompt to attempt a zone transfer?** 
     1.  list server=192.168.10.2 type=all
     2.  is-d abccorp.local
     3.  Iserver 192.168.10.2-t all
     4.  List domain=Abccorp.local type=zone
55.  **How does a denial-of-service attack work?** 
     1.  A hacker prevents a legitimate user (or a group of users) from accessing a service.
     2.  A hacker uses every character, word, or letter he or she can think of to defeat authentication.
     3.  A hacker tries to decipher a password by using a system, which subsequently crashes the network.
     4.  A hacker attempts to imitate a legitimate user by confusing a computer or even another person.
56.  **Message can be sent more securely using DES by ?** 
     1.  Encrypting plain text by a different randomly selected key for each transmission
     2.  Encrypting plain text by a different random key for each message transmission and sending the key to the receiver using a public key system
     3.  Using an algorithm to implement DES instead of using hardware
     4.  Designing DES with high security and not publicizing algorithm used by it
57.  **What command would you enter if you wanted to perform a stealth scan using Nmap?** 
     1.  nmap -sU
     2.  nmap -sS
     3.  snmap -sM
     4.  nmap -sT
58.  **Which of the following acts requires an employer’s standard national numbers to identify them on standard transactions?** 
     1.  SOX
     2.  HIPAA
     3.  DMCA
     4.  PCI-DSS
59.  **Which of the following attacks exploits web page vulnerabilities that allow an attacker to force an unsuspecting user’s browser to send malicious requests they did not intend?** 
     1.  Command Injection Attack
     2.  File Injection Attack
     3.  Cross-site Request Forgery (CSRF)
     4.  Hidden Field Manipulation Attack
60.  **Which of the following DoS tools is used to attack target web applications by starvation of the available sessions on the web server? The tool keeps sessions at halt using never-ending POST transmissions and sending an arbitrarily large content-length header value.** 
     1.  My Doom
     2.  Astacheldraht
     3.  R-U-Dead-Yet?(RUDY)
     4.  LOIC
61.  **Which protocol is used for setting up secure channels between two devices, typically in VPNs?** 
     1.  PPP
     2.  IPSEC
     3.  PEM
     4.  SET
62.  **Firewalls are the software or hardware systems that are able to control and monitor the traffic coming in and out of the target network based on a pre-defined set of rules.**
     
     **Which of the following types of firewalls can protect against SQL injection attacks?**
     
     1.  Data-driven firewall
     2.  Stateful firewall
     3.  Packet firewall
     4.  Web application firewall
63.  **Why are containers less secure than virtual machines?** 
     1.  The host OS on containers has a larger surface attack.
     2.  Containers may fill disk space of the host.
     3.  A compromise container may cause a CPU starvation of the host.
     4.  Containers are attached to the same virtual network.
64.  **A pen tester is configuring a Windows laptop for a test. In setting up Wireshark, what river and library are required to allow the NIC to work in the promiscuous mode?** 
     1.  Libpcap
     2.  Awinpcap
     3.  Winprom
     4.  Winpcap
65.  **Which of the following processes evaluates the adherence of an organization to its stated security policy?** 
     1.  Vulnerability assessment
     2.  Penetration testing
     3.  Risk assessment
     4.  Security auditing
66.  **Which tool would be used to collect the wireless packet data?** 
     1.  NetStumbler
     2.  John the Ripper
     3.  Nessus
     4.  Netcat
67.  **Which of the following examples best represents a logical or technical control?** 
     1.  Security tokens
     2.  Heating and air conditioning
     3.  Smoke and fire alarms
     4.  Corporate security policy
68.  **A security analyst is performing an audit on the network to determine if there are any deviations from the security policies in place. The analyst discovers that a user from the IT department had a dial-out modem installed. Which security policy must the security analyst check to see if dial-out modems are allowed?** 
     1.  Firewall management policy
     2.  Acceptable use policy
     3.  Remote access policy
     4.  Permissive policy
69.  **A developer for a company is tasked with creating a program that will allow customers to update their billing and shipping information. The billing address field used is limited to 50 characters. What pseudo code would the developer use to avoid a buffer overflow attack on the billing address field?** 
     1.  if (billingAddress = 50) {update field} else exit
     2.  if (billingAddress != 50) {update field} else exit
     3.  if (billingAddress >= 50) {update field} else exit
     4.  if (billingAddress <= 50) {update field} else exit
70.  **Which protocol and port number might be needed in order to send log messages to a log analysis tool that resides behind a firewall?** 
     1.  UDP 123
     2.  UDP 541
     3.  UDP 514
     4.  UDP 415
71.  **A hacker, who posed as a heating and air conditioning specialist, was able to install a sniffer program in a switched environment network. Which attack could the hacker use to sniff all of the packets in the network?** 
     1.  Fraggle
     2.  MAC Flood
     3.  Smurf
     4.  Tear Drop
72.  **Which results will be returned with the following Google search query? site:target.com -site:Marketing.target.com accounting** 
     1.  Results matching all the words in the query
     2.  Results matching “accounting” in the domain ‘target.com’ but not on the site ‘Marketing.target.com’
     3.  Results from matches on the site ‘Marketing.target.com’ that are in the domain ‘target.com’ but do not include the word 'accounting'
     4.  Results for matches on ‘target.com’ and ‘Marketing.target.com’ that include the word “accounting”
73.  **The responsibility of a certificate authority for digital signature is to authenticate the** 
     1.  Hash function used
     2.  Public keys for subscribers
     3.  Private keys for subscribers
     4.  Key used in DES
74.  **When an alert rule is matched in a network-based IDS like snort, the IDS does which of the following?** 
     1.  Drops the packet and moves on to the next one
     2.  Continues to evaluate the packet until all the rules are checked
     3.  Stops checking the rules, sends an alert, and lets the packet continue
     4.  Blocks the connection with the source IP address in the packet
75.  **When using Wireshark to acquire a packet capture on a network, which device would enable the capture of all traffic on the wire?** 
     1.  Network tap
     2.  Layer 3 switch
     3.  Network bridge
     4.  Application firewall
76.  **Which of the following is an example of an asymmetric encryption implementation?** 
     1.  SHA1
     2.  PGP
     3.  3DES
     4.  MD5
77.  **Diffie-Hellman (DH) groups determine the strength of the key used in the key exchange process. Which of the following is the correct bit size of the Diffie-Hellman (DH) group 5?** 
     1.  768-bit key
     2.  1025-bit key
     3.  1536-bit key
     4.  2048-bit key
78.  **An Nmap scan of a server shows that port 25 is open. What risk could this pose?** 
     1.  Open printer sharing
     2.  Web portal data leak
     3.  Clear text authentication
     4.  Active mail relay
79.  **The Bluetooth uses which digital modulation technique to exchange information between paired devices?** 
     1.  PSK (phase-shift keying)
     2.  FSK (frequency-shift keying)
     3.  ASK (amplitude-shift keying)
     4.  QAM (quadrature amplitude modulation)
80.  **What statement is true regarding LM hashes?** 
     1.  LM hashes consist of 48 hexadecimal characters.
     2.  LM hashes are based on the AES128 cryptographic standard.
     3.  Upper case characters in the password are converted to lower case.
     4.  LM hashes are not generated when the password length exceeds 15 characters.
81.  **While testing a company's web applications, a tester attempts to insert the following test script into the search area on the company's website:**
     
     `<script>alert(" Testing Testing Testing ")</script>`
     
     **Afterwards, when the tester presses the search button, a pop-up box appears on the screen with the text: "Testing Testing Testing". Which vulnerability has been detected in the web application?**
     
     1.  Buffer overflow
     2.  Cross-site request forgery
     3.  Distributed denial of service
     4.  Cross-site scripting
82.  **Which of the following is an example of IP spoofing?** 
     1.  SQL injections
     2.  Man-in-the-middle
     3.  Cross-site scripting
     4.  ARP poisoning
83.  **Which of the following is a design pattern based on the distinct pieces of software providing application functionality as services to other applications?** 
     1.  Service-oriented architecture
     2.  Object-oriented architecture
     3.  Lean coding
     4.  Agile process
84.  **Open Web Application Security Project (OWASP) is the worldwide not-for-profit charitable organization focused on improving the security of software. What item is the primary concern on OWASP's top ten project most critical web application security risks?** 
     1.  Injection
     2.  Cross-site scripting
     3.  Cross-site request forgery
     4.  Path disclosure
85.  **While using your bank’s online servicing, you notice the following string in the URL bar: “http://www.MyPersonalBank.com/account?id=368940911028389 &Damount=10980&Camount=21”**
     
     **You observe that if you modify the Damount & Camount values and submit the request, that data on the web page reflects the changes. Which type of vulnerability is present on this site?**
     
     1.  Web parameter tampering
     2.  Cookie tampering
     3.  XSS reflection
     4.  SQL injection
86.  **Which of the following areas is considered a strength of symmetric key cryptography when compared with asymmetric algorithms?** 
     1.  Scalability
     2.  Speed
     3.  Key distribution
     4.  Security
87.  **Websites and web portals that provide web services commonly use Simple Object Access Protocol, SOAP. Which of the following is an incorrect definition or characteristics in the protocol?** 
     1.  Based on XML
     2.  Provides a structured model for messaging
     3.  Exchanges data between web services
     4.  Only compatible with the application protocol HTTP
88.  **Due to a slowdown of normal network operations, the IT department decided to monitor the internet traffic of all employees. From a legal stand point, what would be troublesome to take this kind of measure?** 
     1.  All the employees would stop their normal work activities.
     2.  The IT department would be telling employees who the boss is.
     3.  Not informing the employees that they are going to be monitored could be an invasion of privacy.
     4.  The network could still experience a traffic slowdown.
89.  **Which Metasploit Framework tool can help a penetration tester for evading Anti-virus Systems?** 
     1.  msfpayload
     2.  msfcli
     3.  msfencode
     4.  msfd
90.  **In many states, sending spam is illegal. Thus, the spammers have techniques to try and ensure that no one knows they sent the spam out to thousands of users at a time. Which of the following best describes what spammers use to hide the origin of these types of e-mails?** 
     1.  A blacklist of companies that have their mail server relays configured to allow traffic only to their specific domain name.
     2.  Mail relaying, which is a technique of bouncing e-mail from internal to external mail servers continuously.
     3.  A blacklist of companies that have their mail server relays configured to be wide open.
     4.  Tools that will reconfigure a mail server's relay component to send the e-mail back to the spammers occasionally.
91.  **An Internet Service Provider (ISP) has a need to authenticate users connecting using analog modems, Digital Subscriber Lines (DSL), wireless data services, and Virtual Private Networks (VPN) over a Frame Relay network. Which AAA protocol is most likely able to handle this requirement?** 
     1.  RADIUS
     2.  DIAMETER
     3.  Kerberos
     4.  TACACS+
92.  **Ricardo wants to send secret messages to a competitor company. To secure these messages, he uses a technique of hiding a secret message within an ordinary message. The technique provides 'security through obscurity'. What technique is Ricardo using?** 
     1.  Steganography
     2.  Public-key cryptography
     3.  RSA algorithm
     4.  Encryption
93.  **While performing online banking using a web browser, Kyle receives an email that contains an image of a well-crafted art. Upon clicking the image, a new tab on the web browser opens and shows an animated GIF of bills and coins being swallowed by a crocodile. After several days, Kyle notices that all his funds in the bank is gone. Which web browser-based security vulnerability got exploited by the hacker?** 
     1.  Clickjacking
     2.  Web form input validation
     3.  Cross-site request forgery
     4.  Cross-site scripting
94.  **When security and confidentiality of data within the same LAN is of utmost priority, which IPSec mode should you implement?** 
     1.  AH tunnel mode
     2.  AH promiscuous
     3.  ESP transport mode
     4.  ESP confidential
95.  **\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_ is an attack type for a rogue Wi-Fi access point that appears to be a legitimate one offered on the premises, but actually has been set up to eavesdrop on wireless communications. It is a wireless version of the phishing scam. An attacker fools wireless users into connecting a laptop or mobile phone to a tainted hotspot by posing as a legitimate provider. This type of attack may be used to steal the passwords of unsuspecting users by either snooping the communication link or by phishing, which involves setting up a fraudulent website and luring people there.**
     
     **Fill in the blank with the appropriate choice.**
     
     1.  Collision attack
     2.  Evil twin attack
     3.  Sinkhole attack
     4.  Signal jamming attack
96.  **Backing up data is a security must. However, it also has a certain level of risks when mishandled. Which of the following is the greatest threat posed by backups?** 
     1.  A backup is the source of malware or illicit information.
     2.  A backup is incomplete because no verification is performed.
     3.  A backup is unavailable during disaster recovery.
     4.  An unencrypted backup can be misplaced or stolen.
97.  **Knowing the nature of backup tapes, which of the following is the MOST RECOMMENDED way of storing backup tapes?** 
     1.  In a cool dry environment
     2.  Inside the data center for faster retrieval in a fireproof safe
     3.  In an off-site, climate-controlled facility
     4.  On a different floor in the same building
98.  **Which of the following is a vulnerability in GNU’s bash shell (discovered in September, 2014) that gives attackers access to run remote commands on a vulnerable system?** 
     1.  Shellshock
     2.  Rootshell
     3.  Rootshock
     4.  Shellbash
99.  **What is the approximate cost of a replacement and recovery operation per year of a hard drive that has a value of $300 given that the technician who charges $10/hr would need 10 hours to restore the OS and software and needs 4 more hours to restore the database from the last backup to the new hard disk? Calculate the SLE, ARO, and ALE. Assume EF = 1 (100%).** 
     1.  $440
     2.  $100
     3.  $1320
     4.  $146
100.  **Supposed you are the Chief Network Engineer of a certain Telco. Your company is planning a big business expansion and it requires that your network authenticate the users connecting to it using analog modems, Digital Subscriber Lines (DSL), wireless data services, and Virtual Private Networks (VPN) over a Frame Relay network. Which AAA protocol would you implement?** 
      1.  TACACS+
      2.  DIAMETER
      3.  Kerberos
      4.  RADIUS
101.  **If a token and a 4-digit Personal Identification Number (PIN) are used to access a computer system and the token performs off-line checking for the correct PIN, what type of attack is possible?** 
      1.  Birthday
      2.  Brute force
      3.  Man-in-the-middle
      4.  Smurf
102.  **A user on your Windows 2000 network has discovered that he can use L0phtcrack to sniff the SMB exchanges that carry user logons. The user is plugged into a hub with 23 other systems. However, he is unable to capture any logons though he knows that other users are logging in. What do you think is the most likely reason behind this?** 
      1.  There is a NIDS present on that segment.
      2.  Kerberos is preventing it.
      3.  Windows logons cannot be sniffed.
      4.  L0phtcrack only sniffs logons to web servers.
103.  **Which security protocol would you use for wireless connectivity?** 
      1.  No protocol
      2.  WEP
      3.  WPA
      4.  WPA2
104.  **What is a Key Reinstallation Attack?** 
      1.  Posing as a legitimate user in a network to connect to the access point
      2.  Posing as an access point in a wireless network resulting in devices getting connected to the attacker’s system
      3.  Reinstalling the router firmware to reset the access key
      4.  Reinstalling a Wi-Fi device adapter in device to reset access parameters
105.  **What effect does the following command have on a wireless network adapter? airmon-ng start wlan0** 
      1.  It starts the wireless adapter in the ad hoc mode.
      2.  It starts the wireless adapter in the managed mode.
      3.  It starts the wireless adapter in the master mode.
      4.  It starts the wireless adapter in the monitor mode.
106.  **Which of the following commands would you use to start logging the captured network data from the wireless interface wlan0?** 
      1.  airmon-ng wlan0 -w <OUTOUT FILE>
      2.  airodump-ng wlan0 -w <OUTOUT FILE>
      3.  aircrack-ng wlan0 -w <OUTOUT FILE>
      4.  aireplay-ng wlan0 -w <OUTOUT FILE>
107.  **Which of the following commands will perform deauthentication of a device with a specified MAC address connected to a wireless interface?** 
      1.  aireplay-ng -1 1 -a <ACCESS POINT MAC> -c <DEVICE MAC> <INTERFACE>
      2.  aireplay-ng -0 1 -a <ACCESS POINT MAC> -c <DEVICE MAC> <INTERFACE>
      3.  aircrack -ng -1 1 -a <ACCESS POINT MAC> -c <DEVICE MAC> <INTERFACE>
      4.  aircrack -ng -0 1 -a <ACCESS POINT MAC> -c <DEVICE MAC> <INTERFACE>
108.  **There are two authentication schemes that are implemented in the wireless security for non-enterprise solutions. These are Pre-Shared Key based authentication and:** 
      1.  Secret Key based authentication
      2.  Open Authentication
      3.  Private Key based authentication
      4.  Mixed authentication
109.  **You need to transfer files between an isolated file server and your machine but do not want the host machine connected to any access point. What wireless mode would you use to achieve connection with the file server without the involvement of any access point in between?** 
      1.  Ad hoc mode
      2.  Managed mode
      3.  Master mode
      4.  Monitor mode
110.  **Which attack does the following command signify?**
      
      `aireplay-ng -5 –b<BSSID> -h < MAC ADDRESS > <INTERFACE>`
      
      1.  Café-latte attack
      2.  Fragmentation attack
      3.  Fake authentication attack
      4.  Chopchop attack
111.  **Which of the following statements is true?** 
      1.  Wi-Fi consumes less power as compared to Bluetooth.
      2.  Bluetooth supports a large number of users connecting simultaneously.
      3.  Bluetooth requires a high bandwidth.
      4.  Bluetooth and Wi-Fi both use radio signals for communication.
112.  **Which of the following is the correct sequence of the 802.11 packet format?** 
      1.  Frame check sequence > Frame control > Duration/Connection id > Address > Sequence control > Frame body
      2.  Frame check sequence > Frame control > Address > Duration/Connection id > Sequence control > Frame body
      3.  Frame control > Duration/Connection Id > Address > Sequence control > Frame check sequence > Frame body
      4.  Frame control > Duration/Connection Id > Address > Sequence control > Frame body > Frame check sequence
113.  **What command is used to enlist services being run on a Bluetooth device?** 
      1.  sdptool browse <MAC ID>
      2.  l2ping <MAC ID>
      3.  hcitool name <MAC ID>
      4.  hcitool scan
114.  **What does the following command attempt?**
      
      `hciconfig up`
      
      1.  Initializes a Wi-Fi device
      2.  Initializes a LAN adapter
      3.  Initializes a Bluetooth device
      4.  Initializes stateful firewall
115.  **Which of the following statements is true?** 
      1.  WPA is the most advanced encryption protocol for wireless networks.
      2.  Passive wireless LAN detection occurs by listening to all traffic in range.
      3.  Active wireless LAN detection occurs by physically examining the wireless access point.
      4.  A 4-way handshake consists of two authentication and two deauthentication requests.
116.  **Which of the following cannot exist without a host file?** 
      1.  Adware
      2.  Spyware
      3.  Worm
      4.  Virus
117.  **You are trying to perform a DDoS attack on a network whose capacity far exceeds the capacity of your system. Which of the following would you design to achieve the purpose?** 
      1.  Trojan
      2.  Virus
      3.  Botnet
      4.  Bloatware
118.  **What does the following meterpreter command do?**
      
      `keyscan_dump`
      
      1.  Starts keylogger
      2.  Outputs the logged keystrokes
      3.  Stops the keylogger
      4.  Scans the keyboard for missing keys
119.  **Which of the following was not exploited by the Morris worm?** 
      1.  Finger protocol
      2.  Weak passwords
      3.  rexec
      4.  snmp
120.  **Netcat relay can be used for:** 
      1.  Obfuscating the data packets
      2.  Obfuscating the source of attack
      3.  Obfuscating the attack tool
      4.  Obfuscating the attack payload
121.  **You have recently hidden a piece of malicious code in a notepad application implementation. This malicious code gets triggered only after the application has created a hundred new files. What is this arrangement known as?** 
      1.  Counter virus
      2.  Logic bomb
      3.  Time bomb
      4.  Ticker worm
122.  **When a computer boots up, the BIOS looks for a specific sector on the disk to figure out the tasks it needs to invoke the operating system. This is usually the first sector on the disk. What is it called?** 
      1.  Volume Boot Record
      2.  Master Boot Record
      3.  FAT Record
      4.  NTFS Record
123.  **While sniffing a network, you find that some ARP requests have the destination address as ff:ff:ff:ff:ff:ff in the ethernet header. What are these requests being used for?** 
      1.  ARP probe
      2.  ARP announcement
      3.  ARP acknowledgement
      4.  ARP disconnect
124.  **Which of the following commands sends out an ICMP echo request?** 
      1.  Ifcongif/all
      2.  ping <HOSTNAME/IP>
      3.  ipconfig/renew
      4.  echo
125.  **Which of the following is not a valid router command?** 
      1.  router#show class-map
      2.  router#show frame-relay
      3.  router#show icmp
      4.  router#show ntp
